 <td></td> <td>d) other changes to a test procedure, including replacement or addition.</td> <td colspan="2"></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>38.1.</td> <td colspan="5">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="5">1. Appropriate validation studies have been performed in accordance with the scientific guidelines on the quality, safety and efficacy of medicinal products for human use referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.2 of this Regulation and show that the updated test procedure is at least equivalent to the former test procedure.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="5">2. There have been no changes of the total impurity limits. No new unqualified impurities are detected.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="5">3. The method of analysis should remain the same, for example a change in column length or temperature, but not a different type of column or method.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="5">4. The test method is not a biological, immunological and immunochemical method or a method using a biological reagent and it does not include standard pharmacopoeial microbiological methods.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>38.2.</td> <td colspan="5">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="5">1. Amendment of the relevant section of the application dossier, presented in the Common Technical Document format referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.1 of this Regulation, including a description of the analytical methodology, a summary of validation data, revised specifications for impurities, if applicable.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="5">2. Comparative validation results or if justified comparative analysis results showing that the current test and the proposed one are equivalent. This requirement is not applicable in case of an addition of a new test procedure.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>39.</td> <td><b>B.II.d.3 Variations related to the introduction of real-time release or parametric release in the manufacture of the finished product</b>.</td> <td colspan="3"></td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 </table>

2.2.5. B.II. e) Container closure system

<table>
 <tr>
 <td>No</td> <td>Title of the variation. Conditions to be fulfilled. Documentation to be supplied.</td> <td>Conditions to be fulfilled</td> <td>Documentation to be supplied</td> <td>Procedure type</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>40.</td> <td>B.II.e.1 Change in immediate packaging of the finished product:</td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) qualitative and quantitative composition:</td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>1. Solid pharmaceutical forms.</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 3</td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4, 6</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>2. Semi-solid and non-sterile liquid pharmaceutical forms.</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 5, 6</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>3. Sterile medicinal products and biological, including immunological medicinal products.</td> <td colspan="2"></td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>4. The change relates to a less protective pack where there are associated changes in storage conditions and reduction in shelf life.</td> <td colspan="2"></td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) type of container:</td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>1. Solid, semi-solid and non-sterile liquid pharmaceutical forms.</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 5, 6, 7</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>2. Sterile medicinal products and biological, including immunological, medicinal products.</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>40.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. The change only concerns the same packaging type, for example blister to blister.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. The proposed packaging material must be at least equivalent to the approved material in respect of its relevant properties.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">3. Relevant stability studies have been started under ICH conditions included in the scientific guidelines on the quality, safety and efficacy of medicinal products for human use referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.2 of this Regulation and relevant stability parameters have been assessed in at least two pilot scale or industrial scale batches and at least three months satisfactory stability data are at the disposal of the applicant at time of implementation. However, if the proposed packaging is more resistant than the existing packaging, for example thicker blister packaging, the three months' stability data do not yet have to be available. These studies must be finalised and the data will be provided immediately to the competent authorities if outside specifications or potentially outside specifications at the end of the approved shelf life (with proposed action).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>40.2.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Amendments to the relevant section of the application dossier, presented in the Common Technical Document format referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.1 of this Regulation, including revised medicinal product information as appropriate.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Appropriate data on the new packaging (comparative data on permeability, for example for O<sub>2</sub>, CO<sub>2</sub> moisture).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">3. Where appropriate, proof must be provided that no interaction between the content and the packaging material occurs, for example no migration of components of the proposed material into the content and no loss of components of the product into the pack, including confirmation that the material complies with relevant pharmacopoeial requirements or regulatory enactments on materials (plastic) and objects in contact with foodstuffs.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">4. A declaration that the required stability studies have been started under ICH conditions included in the scientific guidelines on the quality, safety and efficacy of medicinal products for human use referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.2 of this Regulation with indication of the batch numbers concerned and that, as relevant, the required minimum satisfactory stability data were at the disposal of the applicant at time of implementation and that the available data did not indicate a problem. Assurance should also be given that the studies will be finalised and that data will be provided immediately to the competent authorities if outside specifications or potentially outside specifications at the end of the approved shelf life (with proposed action).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">5. The results of stability studies that have been carried out under ICH conditions included in the scientific guidelines on the quality, safety and efficacy of medicinal products for human use referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.2 of this Regulation, on the relevant stability parameters, on at least two pilot or industrial scale batches, covering a minimum period of three months, and an assurance is given that these studies will be finalised, and that data will be provided immediately to the competent authorities if outside specifications or potentially outside specifications at the end of the approved shelf life (with proposed action).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">6. Comparative table of the current and proposed immediate packaging specifications for the test and limits of the manufacturing process, if applicable.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">7. Samples of the new container and closure where applicable in accordance with the requirements in respect of the samples collected in the Member States that are included in the recommendations of the European Commission on documentation referred to in Section 28.<sup>1</sup> of the Pharmaceutical Law.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>40.3.</td> <td colspan="4">Note.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">For applicants or variation application referred to in Sub-paragraph 40. b) of this Annex are reminded that any change which results in a "new pharmaceutical form" requires the submission of an Extension application.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>41.</td> <td>B.II.e.2 Change in the specification parameters and limits of the immediate packaging of the finished product:</td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) tightening of specification limits;</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4</td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) addition of a new specification parameter to the specification with its corresponding test method;</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 5</td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4, 6</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>c) deletion of a non-significant specification parameter, for example deletion of an obsolete parameter;</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2</td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 5</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>d) addition or replacement of a specification parameter as a result of a safety or quality issue.</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4, 6</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>41.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. The change is not a consequence of any commitment from previous assessments to review specification limits, for example made during the procedure for the registration application or a type II variation procedure.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. The change does not result from unexpected events arising during manufacture.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">3. Any change should be within the range of currently approved limits.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">4. The test procedure remains the same, or changes in the test procedure are minor.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">5. Any new test method does not concern a novel non-standard technique or a standard technique used in a novel way.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>41.2.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Amendments to the relevant section of the application dossier, presented in the Common Technical Document format referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.1 of this Regulation.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Comparative table of current and proposed specifications.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">3. Details of any new analytical method and validation data, where relevant.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">4. Batch analysis data on two batches of the immediate packaging for all specification parameters.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">5. Justification and risk-assessment showing that the parameter is non-significant.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">6. Justification of the new specification parameter and the limits.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>42.</td> <td><b>B.II.e.3 Change in test procedure for the immediate packaging of the finished product</b>:</td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) minor changes to an approved test procedure;</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 3</td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) other changes to a test procedure, including replacement or addition;</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 3, 4</td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>c) deletion of a test procedure if an alternative test procedure is already authorised.</td> <td>Condition: 5</td> <td>Documentation: 1</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>42.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Appropriate validation studies have been performed in accordance with the scientific guidelines on the quality, safety and efficacy of medicinal products for human use referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.2 of this Regulation and validation studies show that the updated test procedure is at least equivalent to the former test procedure.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. The method of analysis should remain the same, for example a change in column length or temperature, but not a different type of column or method.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">3. Any new test method does not concern a novel non-standard technique or a standard technique used in a novel way.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">4. The active substance and finished product is not biological, including immunological.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">5. An alternative test procedure is already authorised for the specification parameter and this procedure has not been added through notification regarding IA and IA<sub>IN</sub> type variations.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>42.2.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Amendments to the relevant section of the application dossier, presented in the Common Technical Document format referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.1 of this Regulation, including a description of the analytical methodology and a summary of validation data.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Comparative validation results or if justified comparative analysis results showing that the current test and the proposed one are equivalent. This requirement is not applicable in case of an addition of a new test procedure.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>43.</td> <td><b>B.II.e.4 Change in shape or dimensions of the container or closure (immediate packaging)</b>:</td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) non-sterile medicinal products;</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 3</td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 4</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) the change in shape or dimensions concerns a fundamental part of the packaging material, which may have a significant impact on the delivery, use, safety or stability of the finished product;</td> <td colspan="2"></td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>c) sterile medicinal products.</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>43.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. No change in the qualitative or quantitative composition of the container.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. The change does not concern a fundamental part of the packaging material, which affects the delivery<b>,</b> use, safety or stability of the finished product.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">3. In case of a change in the headspace or a change in the surface and volume ratio, stability studies in accordance with the scientific guidelines on the quality, safety and efficacy of medicinal products for human use referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.2 of this Regulation have been started and relevant stability parameters have been assessed in at least two pilot scale (three for biological, including immunological medicinal products) or industrial scale batches and at least three months, but six months for biological, including immunological, medicinal products, stability data are at the disposal of the applicant. Assurance is given that these studies will be finalised and that data will be provided immediately to the competent authorities if outside specifications or potentially outside specifications at the end of the approved shelf life (with proposed action).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>43.2.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Amendments to the relevant section of the application dossier, presented in the Common Technical Document format referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.1 of this Regulation, including description and detailed drawing of the container or closure material, and including revised medicinal product information as appropriate.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Samples of the new container/closure where applicable in accordance with the requirements for samples collected in the Member States that are determined in the recommendations of the European Commission on documentation referred to in Section 28.<sup>1</sup> of the Pharmaceutical Law.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">3. Re-validation studies have been performed in case of sterile products terminally sterilised. The batch numbers of the batches used in the re-validation studies should be indicated, where applicable.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">4. In case of a change in the headspace or a change in the surface and volume ratio, a declaration that the required stability studies have been started under ICH conditions included in the scientific guidelines on the quality, safety and efficacy of medicinal products for human use referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.2 of this Regulation with indication of the batch numbers concerned and that, as relevant, the required minimum satisfactory stability data were at the disposal of the applicant at time of implementation for a Type IA notification and time of submission of a Type IB notification, and that the available data did not indicate a problem. Assurance should also be given that the studies will be finalised and that data will be provided immediately to the competent authorities if outside specifications or potentially outside specifications at the end of the approved shelf life (with proposed action).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>44.</td> <td><b>B.II.e.5 Change in pack size of the finished product</b>:</td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>d) change in the fill weight-fill volume of nonparenteral multi-dose (or single-dose, partial use) products.</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>44.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Justification for the new and remaining pack-size, showing that the new and remaining size is/are consistent with the dosage regimen and duration of use as approved in the summary of product characteristics.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">3. Declaration that stability studies will be conducted in accordance with the scientific guidelines on the quality, safety and efficacy of medicinal products for human use referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.2 of this Regulation for products where stability parameters could be affected. Data to be reported only if outside specifications (with proposed action).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>44.3.</td> <td colspan="4">Note.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">For Paragraph 44, Sub-paragraphs c) and d) of this Annex, applicants are reminded that any changes to the 'strength' of the medicinal product require the submission of an Extension application.</td> </tr>

 <td>48.</td> <td><b>B.II.g.1</b> <b>Introduction of a new design space or extension of an approved design space for the finished product (excluding biologicals) concerning:</b></td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) one or more unit operations in the manufacturing process of the finished product including the resulting in-process controls and test procedures;</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3</td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) test procedures for excipients, intermediates and the finished product.</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3</td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>48.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Results from product and process development studies, including risk assessment and multivariate studies, as appropriate, demonstrating that a systematic mechanistic understanding of material attributes and process parameters to the critical quality attributes of the finished product has been achieved.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Description of the design space in tabular format, including the variables, material attributes and process parameters, as appropriate, and their proposed ranges.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">3. Amendments to the relevant section of the application dossier, presented in the Common Technical Document format referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.1 of this Regulation.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>49.</td> <td>B.II.g.2 Introduction of a post approval change management protocol related to the finished product.</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2</td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>49.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Detailed description for the proposed change.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Change management protocol related to the finished product.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>50.</td> <td><b>B.II.g.3</b> <b>Deletion of an approved change management protocol related to the finish product.</b></td> <td>Condition: 1</td> <td>Documentation: 1</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>50.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Condition:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. The deletion of the approved change management protocol related to the finish product is not a result of unexpected events or out of specification results during the implementation of the changes described in the protocol.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>50.2.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Justification for the proposed deletion.</td> </tr>
 </table>

2.3. B.III. European Pharmacopoeial Certificate of Suitability, TSE Certificate of Suitability and Pharmacopoeial Monographs

<table>
 <tr>
 <td>No</td> <td colspan="2">Title of the variation. Conditions to be fulfilled. Documentation to be supplied.</td> <td>Conditions to be fulfilled</td> <td colspan="2">Documentation to be supplied</td> <td>Procedure type</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>51.</td> <td colspan="2">B.III.1. Submission of a new or updated European Pharmacopoeial Certificate of Suitability for an active substance, for a starting material, reagent and intermediate used in the manufacturing process of the active substance, and for an excipient:</td> <td colspan="4"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="2">a) European Pharmacopoeial Certificate of Suitability to the relevant European Pharmacopoeial Monograph:</td> <td colspan="4"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="2">1. New certificate from an already approved manufacturer.</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 8</td> <td colspan="2">Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="2">2. Updated certificate from an already approved manufacturer.</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4, 8</td> <td colspan="2">Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="2">3. New certificate from a new manufacturer (replacement or addition).</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 8</td> <td colspan="2">Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="2">b) European Pharmacopoeial TSE Certificate of suitability for an active substance, starting material, reagent, intermediate and excipient;</td> <td colspan="4"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="2">1. New certificate for an active substance from a new or an already approved manufacturer.</td> <td>Conditions: 3, 6</td> <td colspan="2">Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="2">2. New certificate for a new or an already approved manufacturer.</td> <td>Conditions: 3, 6</td> <td colspan="2">Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="2">3. Updated certificate from an already approved manufacturer.</td> <td>Conditions: 7</td> <td colspan="2">Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>51.1.</td> <td colspan="6">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">1. The finished product release and end of shelf life specifications remain the same.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">2. Unchanged (excluding tightening) additional (to European Pharmacopoeia) specifications for impurities (excluding residual solvents, provided they are in compliance with scientific guidelines referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.2 of this Regulation) and product specific requirements (for example particle size profiles, polymorphic form), if applicable.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">3. The manufacturing process of the active substance, starting material, reagent and intermediate does not include the use of materials of human or animal origin for which an assessment of viral safety data is required.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">4. For active substance only, it will be tested immediately prior to use if no retest period is included in the European Pharmacopoeial Certificate of Suitability or if data to support a retest period is not already provided in the application dossier.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">5. The active substance, starting material, reagent, intermediate and excipient is not sterile.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">6. For herbal active substances the manufacturing route, physical form, extraction solvent and drug extract ratio should remain the same.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>51.2.</td> <td colspan="6">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">1. Copy of the current European Pharmacopoeial Certificate of Suitability, and if the Certificate is updated, the copy thereof.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">2. In case of an addition of a manufacturing site, the variation application form should clearly outline the present and proposed manufacturers.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">3. Amendments to the relevant section of the application dossier, presented in the Common Technical Document format referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.1 of this Regulation.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">4. Where applicable, a document providing information of any materials falling within the scope of the guidance referred to in Paragraph 32 of Annex 3 to this Regulation on minimising the risk of transmitting animal spongiform encephalopathy agents via medicinal products including materials which are used in the manufacture of the active substance and excipient. The following information should be included for each such material: Name of manufacturer, species and tissues from which the material is a derivative, country of origin of the source animals and its use. For the Centralised Procedure, this information should be included in an updated TSE table A (and B, if relevant).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">5. For active substance - declarations by the Qualified Persons determined by the manufacturing authorisation holders listed in the application where the active substance is used as a starting material and declarations by the Qualified Persons by the manufacturing authorisation holders listed in the application as responsible for batch release. These declarations should state that the active substance manufacturer referred to in the application operate in compliance with the requirements on good manufacturing practice for starting materials in accordance with the determined in the legislation of the European Union and regulatory enactments regarding procedure for manufacture and control of medicinal products. A single declaration may be acceptable under certain circumstances as referred to in Paragraph 29 of this Regulation. The manufacture of intermediates also require a Qualified Person declaration, while as far as any updates to certificates for active substances and intermediates are concerned, a Qualified Person declaration is only required if, compared to the previously registered version of the certificate, there is a change to the actual listed manufacturing sites.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>52.</td> <td>B.III.2. Change to comply with European Pharmacopoeia or with a national pharmacopoeia of a Member State:</td> <td colspan="5"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) change of specification of a former non Pharmacopoeial substance to comply with the European Pharmacopoeia or with a national pharmacopoeia of a Member State:</td> <td colspan="5"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>1. Active substance.</td> <td colspan="2">Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5</td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5</td> <td colspan="2">IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>2. Excipient and active substance starting material.</td> <td colspan="2">Conditions: 1, 2, 4</td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5</td> <td colspan="2">IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) change to comply with an update of the relevant monograph of the European Pharmacopoeia or national pharmacopoeia of a Member State;</td> <td colspan="2">Conditions: 1, 2, 4, 5</td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4</td> <td colspan="2">IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>c) change in specifications from a national pharmacopoeia of a Member State to the European Pharmacopoeia.</td> <td colspan="2">Conditions: 1, 4, 5</td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4</td> <td colspan="2">IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>52.1.</td> <td colspan="6">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">1. The change is made exclusively to comply with the pharmacopoeia.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">2. Additional specifications (in addition to Pharmacopoeia) to the pharmacopoeia for product specific properties are unchanged (for example, particle size profiles, polymorphic form or bioassays, aggregates).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">3. No significant changes in qualitative and quantitative impurities profile unless the specifications are tightened.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">4. Additional validation of a new or changed pharmacopoeial method is not required.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">5. For herbal active substances: the manufacturing route, physical form, extraction solvent and drug extract ratio should remain the same.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>52.2.</td> <td colspan="6">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">1. Amendments to the relevant section of the application dossier, presented in the Common Technical Document format referred to in Sub-paragraph 23.1 of this Regulation.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">2. Comparative table of current and proposed specifications.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">3. Batch analysis data on two production batches of the relevant substance for all tests in the new specification<b>.</b></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">4. Data to demonstrate the suitability of the monograph to control the substance, for example a comparison of the potential impurities with the transparency note of the monograph.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">5. Where appropriate, batch analysis data (in a comparative tabulated format) on two production batches of the finished product containing the substance complying with the current and proposed specification and additionally, where appropriate, comparative dissolution profile data for the finished product on at least one pilot batch. For herbal medicinal products, comparative disintegration data may be acceptable.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>52.3.</td> <td colspan="6">Note:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="6">There is no need to notify the competent authorities of an updated monograph of the European Pharmacopoeia or a national pharmacopoeia of a Member State in the case that compliance with the updated monograph is implemented within six months of its publication and reference is made to the 'current edition' in the application dossier of an authorised medicinal product.</td> </tr>
 </table>

 <tr>

Samples of the new device where applicable in accordance with the requirements for samples collected in the Member States that are determined in the recommendations of the European Commission on documentation referred to in Section 28.<sup>1</sup> of the Pharmaceutical Law.</td> </tr>

 <tr>
 <td>No</td> <td>Title of the variation. Conditions to be fulfilled. Documentation to be supplied.</td> <td>Conditions to be fulfilled</td> <td>Documentation to be supplied</td> <td>Procedure type</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>58.</td> <td>C.I.1 Change in the Summary of Product Characteristics, Labelling or Package Leaflet following a referral procedure in accordance with Sub-paragraph 108.8 of this Regulation:</td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) the medicinal product is covered by the defined scope of the referral;</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) the medicinal product is not covered by the defined scope of the referral but the change implements the outcome of the referral and no new additional data are submitted by the owner of the registration;</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>

 <td>58.2.</td> <td colspan="4">Note:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">Paragraph 58, Sub-paragraph a) of this Annex applies in cases where the owner of the registration need to take steps to allow the Member States of the European Economic Area to ensure the compliance with the European Commission decision on the referral procedure within 30 days after its notification in accordance with Sub-paragraph 108.8 of this Regulation.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>59.</td> <td>C.I.2. Change in the Summary of Product Characteristics, Labelling or Package Leaflet of a Generic, hybrid and biosimilar medicinal products following assessment of the same change for the reference product:</td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) implementation of changes for which no new additional data are submitted by the owner of the registration;</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) implementation of change which require to be further substantiated by new additional data to be submitted by the owner of the registration (for example comparability).</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>59.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Attached to the cover letter of the variation application: European Medicines Agency or competent authority request, if applicable.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Revised medicinal product information.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>60.</td> <td>C.I.3. Implementation of changes requested by the European Medicines Agency or competent authority following the assessment of an Urgent Safety Restriction, class labelling, a Periodic Safety Update report, Risk Management Plan, Follow Up Measure and Specific Obligation, data submitted under Article 45 and 46 of Regulation (EC) No 1901/2006 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 12 December 2006 on medicinal products for paediatric use and amending Regulation (EEC) No 1768/92, Directive 2001/20/EC, Directive 2001/83/EC and Regulation (EC) No726/2004, or amendments to reflect a competent authority core requirements of a supplementary protection certificate:</td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) implementation of agreed wording changes for which no new additional data are submitted by the owner of the registration;</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) implementation of changes which require to be further substantiated by new additional data to be submitted by the owner of the registration.</td> <td colspan="2"></td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>60.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Attached to the cover letter of the variation application: European Medicines Agency or competent authority request, if available.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Revised medicinal product information.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>60.2.</td> <td colspan="4">Notes.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">The owners of the registration are reminded that once new information becomes available which might entail the variation of the application dossier, this should be submitted forthwith as a variation to the competent authorities, rather than awaiting the assessment of those data through one of the procedures mentioned above.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>61.</td> <td><b>C.I.4.</b> <b>Variations related to significant modifications of the Summary of Product Characteristics due in particular to new quality, pre-clinical, clinical or pharmacovigilance data.</b></td> <td colspan="2"></td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>62.</td> <td>C.I.5. Change in the legal status of a medicinal product for centrally authorised products:</td> <td colspan="2"></td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) for generic, hybrid, biosimilar medicinal products following an approved legal status change of the reference medicinal product;</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) all other legal status changes.</td> <td colspan="2"></td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>62.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Attached to the cover letter of the variation application: proof of authorisation of the legal status change (for example reference to the Commission Decision concerned).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Revised medicinal product information.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>62.2.</td> <td colspan="4">Note.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">For nationally authorised products approved via mutual recognition procedure and decentralised procedure, the change of the legal status is to be handled at national level (not via a mutual recognition procedure variation).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>63.</td> <td><b>C.I.6.</b> <b>Changes to therapeutic indications:</b></td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) addition of a new therapeutic indication or modification of an approved one;</td> <td colspan="2"></td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) deletion of a therapeutic indication.</td> <td colspan="2"></td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>63.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Notes.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">Where the addition or modification of a therapeutic indication takes place in the context of the implementation of the outcome of a referral procedure or of changes to the product information of a generic, hybrid and biosimilar product following assessment of the same change for the reference product, variations referred to in Paragraphs 58 and 59 of this Annex apply, respectively.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>64.</td> <td>C.I.7. Deletion of:</td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) a pharmaceutical form;</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) a strength.</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>64.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Declaration that the remaining product presentation are adequate for the dosing instructions and treatment duration as mentioned in the summary of product characteristics.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Revised medicinal product information.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>64.2.</td> <td colspan="4">Note:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">Where a given pharmaceutical form or strength has received a marketing authorization which is separate to the marketing authorization for other pharmaceutical forms or strengths, the deletion of the former will not be a variation but the withdrawal of the marketing authorization.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>65.</td> <td>C.I.8. Introduction of a new pharmacovigilance system:</td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) which has not been assessed by the competent authority or European Medicines Agency for another product of the same owner of the registration;</td> <td colspan="2"></td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) which has been assessed by the competent authority or European Medicines Agency for another product of the same owner of the registration.</td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>65.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. The detailed description of the new pharmacovigilance system.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>65.2.</td> <td colspan="4">Note:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">The variations referred to in Paragraph 65, Sub-paragraph b) of this Annex covers the situation where the applicability of an already assessed pharmacovigilance system will have to be assessed for the new marketing authorisations concerned (at time of transfer of a marketing authorisation).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>66.</td> <td>C.I.9. Changes to an existing pharmacovigilance system for as described in the detailed description of the pharmacovigilance system:</td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>a) change in the appropriately qualified person responsible for the pharmacovigilance;</td> <td>Conditions: 1</td> <td>Documentation: 1</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>b) change in the contact details of the appropriately qualified person responsible for the pharmacovigilance;</td> <td>Conditions: 1</td> <td>Documentation: 2</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>c) change of the back-up procedure of the appropriately qualified person responsible for the pharmacovigilance;</td> <td>Conditions: 1</td> <td>Documentation: 2</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>d) change in the safety database (for example introduction of a new safety database including safety data collection and analysis and reporting to the new system);</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 3</td> <td>Documentation: 2</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>e) changes in the major contractual arrangements with other persons or institutions and authorities involved in the fulfilment of obligations described in the pharmacovigilance system, in particular where the electronic reporting of adverse effects caused by use of particular medicinal products, the main databases, signal detection, or periodic safety update reports is subcontracted;</td> <td>Conditions: 1</td> <td>Documentation: 2</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>f) deletion of topics covered by written procedure describing the activities of the pharmacovigilance system;</td> <td>Conditions: 1</td> <td>Documentation: 2</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>g) change of the site undertaking the activities of the pharmacovigilance system;</td> <td>Conditions: 1</td> <td>Documentation: 2</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>h) other changes to the pharmacovigilance system that does not impact on the operation thereof (for example change of the major storage and archiving location, administrative changes, update of acronyms, naming changes of functions and procedures).</td> <td>Conditions: 1</td> <td>Documentation: 2</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>i) changes to the pharmacovigilance system following the assessment of the same pharmacovigilance system in relation to another medicinal product of the same owner of the registration.</td> <td>Conditions: 4</td> <td>Documentation: 2, 3</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>66.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. The pharmacovigilance system itself remains unchanged.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. The database system has been validated (approved).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">3. Transfer of data from other database systems has been validated (approved).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">4. The same changes to the pharmacovigilance system are introduced for all medicinal products of the same owner of the registration. The same final version of the pharmacovigilance system.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>66.2.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">Latest version of the pharmacovigilance system, including a) summary (<i>curriculum vitae</i>) of work experience (life) of the new appropriately qualified person responsible for pharmacovigilance;<br> b) proof that an appropriately qualified person responsible for the pharmacovigilance is registered in EudraVigilance database;<br> c) a new statement signed by the owner of the registration and the appropriately qualified person responsible for the pharmacovigilance regarding their availability and the means for notification of adverse reactions, and reflecting any other consequential changes, for example to the organisation chart.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Latest version of the pharmacovigilance system description and/or latest version of product specific addendum. For variation referred to in Paragraph 66, Sub-paragraph b) of this Annex if the contact details of the appropriately qualified person responsible for pharmacovigilance were not initially included in the pharmacovigilance system, submission of a revised version of the pharmacovigilance system description is not required, only variation application form or notification to be provided.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">3. Reference of the application and procedure and medicinal product in which the changes were accepted.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>66.3.</td> <td colspan="4">Note:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">For the assessment of a pharmacovigilance system referred to in Paragraph 66, Sub-paragraph i) of this Annex and submitted as part of a new medicinal product registration or extension or variation application may give rise to changes at the request of the national competent authority or European Medicines Agency in this pharmacovigilance system description. Where this occurs, the same changes can be introduced to the pharmacovigilance system in other marketing authorisations of the same owner of the registration by submitting a (grouped) Type IAIN variation.</td> </tr>
 </table>

 <tr>
 <td>69.</td> <td><b>D.3</b> <b>Change or transfer of the current PMF certificate holder to a new PMF certificate holder, namely different legal entity.</b></td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6</td> <td>IA<sub>IN</sub></td> </tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td>70.</td> <td><b>D.4</b> <b>Change in the name and address of a blood centre including centre and establishment (structural unit thereof) responsible for collection of blood and plasma</b></td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2</td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>70.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. The relevant centre and establishment shall remain the same legal entity.</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Signed declaration that the change does not involve a change of the quality system within the relevant centre and establishment.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Signed declaration that there is no change in the list of the blood and plasma collection centres.</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td>71.</td> <td><b>D.5</b> <b>Replacement or addition of a structural unit of blood and plasma collection within a blood centre and establishment already included in the PMF.</b></td> <td></td> <td>Documentation: 1, 2, 3</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>71.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Epidemiological data for viral markers related to the blood and plasma collection centres and establishments (structural units thereof) covering the last 3 years.  <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Statement that the blood and plasma collection centre and establishment (structural unit thereof) is working under the same conditions as the other centres and establishments (structural units thereof) belonging to the same centre and establishment (structural unit thereof), as specified in the standard contract between such centre and establishment (structural unit thereof) and PMF holder.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">3. Updated relevant sections and annexes of the PMF application dossier.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>72.</td> <td><b>D.6</b> <b>Deletion or change of status (operational, nonoperational) of establishment (structural unit thereof) and centre used for blood and plasma collection or in the testing of donations and plasma pools.</b></td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2</td> <td>Documentation: 1</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>72.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. The reason for deletion or change of status should not be related to a good manufacturing practice issue.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. If non-operational establishment (structural unit thereof) and centre becomes as operational establishment (structural unit thereof) and centre, the establishment (structural unit thereof) and centre should comply with the legislation in terms of inspections determined in the regulatory enactment regarding collection, testing, treatment, storage and distribution of human blood and blood components in case of change of status.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>72.2.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Updated relevant sections and annexes of the PMF application dossier.</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td>74.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. Updated relevant sections and annexes of the PMF application dossier.</td> </tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td>79.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <td></td> <td colspan="4">2. Updated relevant sections and annexes of the PMF application dossier.</td> </tr>

 <tr>
 <td>80.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. The new test kit is CE-marked. .</td> </tr>

 <tr>
 <td>81.</td> <td><b>D.15</b> <b>Addition of a non-CE marked test kit to test individual donations as a new test kit or as a replacement of an existing test kit:</b></td> <td colspan="3"></td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td>82.</td> <td><b>D.16</b> <b>Change of kit and method used to test pools (antibody or antigen or NAT test).</b></td> <td colspan="2"></td> <td>II</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>83.</td> <td><b>D.17</b> <b>Introduction or extension of inventory hold procedure.</b></td> <td>Conditions: 1</td> <td>Documentation: 1</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>83.1.</td> <td colspan="4">Conditions:</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="4">1. The inventory hold procedure is a more stringent procedure (for example release only after retesting of donors).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>83.2.</td> <td colspan="4">Documentation:</td> </tr>
 <tr>

Notes.

1. "Information on medicinal product" referred to in this Annex means a description, labelling or package leaflet of medicinal product.

2. "Test procedure" referred to in this Annex means the same as "analytical procedure", and "restrictions" means the same as "approval criteria".

3. "Specification parameter" referred to in this Annex means a quality value for which a test procedure and restrictions are determined, for example assay, identity, water content. Addition or deletion of a specification parameter shall include the relevant test method and restrictions thereof.

4. "Amendments of the relevant section of the application dossier" referred to in this Annex means addition, replacement or deletion of data and documents unless provided otherwise. If the amendments of the relevant section of the application dossier apply only to editorial amendments, such amendments shall not be provided as separate amendments, but they may be included in amendments that apply to a particular section of the application dossier. In such cases a person who notifies regarding amendments shall attest that the content of the relevant section of the application dossier has not been changed as a result of amendments of editorial nature, and that it does not exceed the essence of the content of the submitted amendments.

[28 July 2008; 18 May 2010; 9 April 2013]

**Annex 8**
Cabinet Regulation No. 376
9 May 2006

## Minor Variations of Type IA and IB

Minor variations of type I A and I B for medicinal products registered in accordance with a national registration procedure (shall not apply to medicinal products registered in accordance with a mutual recognition procedure and decentralised procedure) shall be as follows:

<table>
 <tr>
 <td>No.</td> <td colspan="4">Title of the variation. Conditions to be fulfilled</td> <td>Type</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="2">1.</td> <td colspan="4">Change in the name or address of the owner of the medicinal product registration certificate</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td rowspan="2">2.</td> <td colspan="4">Change in the name of the medicinal product</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td rowspan="2">3.</td> <td colspan="4">Change in the name of the active substance</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Condition: change following granting of or amendment to the ATC code by the World Health Organisation</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td colspan="2">7.1. secondary packaging for all types of pharmaceutical forms</td> <td colspan="2">Conditions: 1, 2 (see below)</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>

 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The relevant state competent authority of one of the Member States of the European Economic Area or a country, which has a mutual recognition agreement with the European Communities regarding good manufacturing practice, has carried out a satisfactory inspection in the last 3 years.<br> 2. The site of manufacture has an appropriate licence (a permit to manufacture the pharmaceutical form of the relevant product).<br> 3. The relevant product is not a sterile product.<br> 4. A validation scheme is available or validation of the manufacture at the new site has been successfully carried out according to the current protocol with at least 3 production scale batches.<br> 5. The relevant product is not a biological medicinal product.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="6">8.</td> <td colspan="5">Changes in the batch release arrangements and quality control testing of the finished product</td> </tr>

 <tr>
 <td>11.1. scale-up up to 10-fold compared to the original batch size approved at the grant of registration</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4 (see below)</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>

The change does not affect the reproducibility of the process.<br> 5. The change shall not be the result of unexpected events arising during manufacture or because of stability concerns.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="7">12.</td> <td colspan="5">Change in the specification of an active substance or a starting material/intermediate/reagent used in the manufacturing process of the active substance</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The change is not a consequence of any commitment from previous assessments to review specification limits (for example, made during the procedure for registration application or a Type II variation procedure).<br> 2. The change shall not be the result of unexpected events arising during manufacture.<br> 3. Any change shall be within the range of currently approved limits.<br> 4. Any new test method does not concern a non-approved technique or a technique used in a novel way.<br> 5. The active substance is not a biological substance.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="4">13.</td> <td colspan="5">Change in the test procedure for the active substance or starting material, intermediate or reagent used in the manufacturing process of the active substance</td> </tr>

 <tr>
 <td>13.2. other changes to a test procedure, also replacement or addition of a test procedure</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 2, 3, 4, 5</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The method of analysis shall remain the same (for example, change in the column length or temperature shall be permissible, but not a different type of the column or method). No new impurities are detected.<br> 2. Appropriate (re-)validation studies have been performed in accordance with the relevant guidelines.<br> 3. Results of method validation show the new test method to be at least equivalent to the former procedure.<br> 4. Any new test method does not concern a non-approved technique or a technique used in a novel way.<br> 5. The active substance, starting material, intermediate or reagent is not a biological substance.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="4">14.</td> <td colspan="5">Change in the manufacture of the active substance or starting material/reagent/intermediate in the manufacturing process of the active substance where no European Pharmacopoeial certificate of suitability is available</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>14.1. change in the site of the already approved manufacturer (replacement or addition of a new site)</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 2, 4 (see below)</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>14.2. new manufacturer (replacement or addition of a new manufacturer)</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The specifications (also in-process controls, methods of analysis of all materials), method of preparation (also batch size) and detailed route of synthesis are identical to those already approved.<br> 2. Where materials of human or animal origin are used in the process, the manufacturer does not use any new supplier, for which assessment is required of the viral safety or of compliance with the European Commission Note for Guidance on minimising the risk of transmitting animal spongiform encephalopathy agents via human and veterinary medicinal products (to the last revision).<br> 3. The current or new active substance manufacturer does not use a Drug Master File.<br> 4. The change does not concern a medicinal product containing a biological active substance.</td> </tr>
 <tr>

The manufacturing process of the active substance, starting material/reagent/intermediate does not include the use of materials of human or animal origin, for which an assessment of viral safety data is required.</td> </tr>

 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The excipient has the same functional characteristics.<br> 2. The dissolution profile of the new product determined on a minimum of two pilot scale batches is comparable to the old one (no significant differences regarding comparability, see <i>Note for Guidance on bioavailability and bioequivalence</i> (Annex II); the principles contained in this note for guidance for medicinal products for human use shall still be taken into account for veterinary medicinal products if relevant). For herbal medicinal products where dissolution testing may not be feasible, the disintegration time of the new product is comparable to the old one.<br> 3. Any new excipient does not include the use of materials of human or animal origin, for which assessment is required of the viral safety data.<br> 4. It does not concern a medicinal product containing a biological active substance.<br> 5. Stability studies in accordance with the relevant guidelines have been started with at least 2 pilot scale or industrial scale batches and at least 3 months satisfactory stability data are at the disposal of the applicant, as well as the assurance that these studies will be finalised. Data shall be provided immediately to the competent authorities if they do not comply, or possibly do not comply with the specifications at the end of the approved shelf life (with the proposed action).</td> </tr>

 <td>27.2. other changes to a test procedure, including replacement or addition of a test procedure</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 2, 3, 4</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The method of analysis shall remain the same (for example, change in column length or temperature shall be permissible, but not a different type of column or method).<br> 2. Appropriate (re-)validation studies were performed in accordance with the relevant guidelines.<br> 3. Results of method validation show the new test method to be at least equivalent to the former procedure.<br> 4. Any new test method does not concern a non-approved technique or a technique used in a novel way.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="2">28.</td> <td colspan="4">Change in any part of the (primary) packaging material not in contact with the finished product formulation (such as the colour of flip-off caps, colour code rings on ampoules, change of needle shield (different plastic used)).</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Condition: the change does not concern a fundamental part of the packaging material, which affects the delivery, use, safety or stability of the finished product.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="5">29.</td> <td colspan="5">Change in the qualitative and/or quantitative composition of the immediate packaging material</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>29.1. soft (semi-liquid) or liquid pharmaceutical forms</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4 (see below)</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="2">29.2. all other pharmaceutical forms</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 3, 4</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The product concerned is not a biological or sterile product.<br> 2. The change concerns only the same packaging type and material (for example, blister to blister).<br> 3. The proposed packaging material shall be at least equivalent to the approved material in respect of the relevant properties thereof.<br> 4. Relevant stability studies in accordance with the relevant guidelines have been started with at least 2 pilot scale or industrial scale batches and at least 3 months stability data are at the disposal of the applicant of the request. Assurance is given that these studies will be finalised and that the data will be provided immediately to the competent authorities if they do not comply, or possibly do not comply with the specifications at the end of the approved shelf life (with the proposed action).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="4">30.</td> <td colspan="5">Change (replacement, addition or deletion) in the supplier of packaging components or devices (when mentioned in the documentation), except spacer devices for metered dose inhalers</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="3">30.1. deletion of a supplier</td> <td>Conditions: 1 (see below)</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="3">30.2. replacement or addition of a supplier</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. No deletion of the packaging component or device.<br> 2. The qualitative and quantitative composition of the packaging components or device remains the same.<br> 3. The specifications and quality control method are at least equivalent.<br> 4. The sterilisation method and conditions remain the same (if applicable).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="5">31.</td> <td colspan="5">Change to in-process tests or limits applied during the manufacture of the medicinal product</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="3">31.1. tightening of in-process limits</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 3 (see below)</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="3" rowspan="2">31.2. addition of new tests and limits</td> <td>Conditions: 2, 3</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>Conditions: 2, 4</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The change is not a consequence of any commitment from previous assessments (for example, made during the procedure for registration request or a type II variation procedure).<br> 2. The change shall not be the result of unexpected events arising during manufacture or because of stability concerns.<br> 3. Any change shall be within the range of currently approved limits.<br> 4. Any new test method does not concern a non-approved technique or a technique used in a novel way.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="5">32.</td> <td colspan="5">Change in the batch size of the finished product</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="3">32.1. scale-up up to 10-fold compared to the original batch size approved at the grant of registration</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5 (see below)</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="3">32.2. downscaling down to 10-fold</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="3">32.3. other cases</td> <td>Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The change does not affect the reproducibility and/or consistency of the product.<br> 2. The change relates only to standard immediate release oral pharmaceutical forms and to non-sterile liquid forms.<br> 3. Any changes to the manufacturing method, as well as to the in-process controls are only those necessitated by the change in batch-size (for example, different sized equipment).<br> 4. A validation scheme is available or validation of the manufacture has been successfully carried out according to the current protocol with at least 3 batches at the proposed new batch size in accordance with the relevant guidelines.<br> 5. It does not concern a medicinal product containing a biological active substance.<br> 6. The change shall not be the result of unexpected events arisen during manufacture or because of stability concerns.<br> 7. Relevant stability studies in accordance with the relevant guidelines have been started with at least 2 pilot scale or industrial scale batches and at least 3 months stability data are at the disposal of the applicant. Assurance is given that these studies will be finalised and that the data will be provided immediately to the competent authorities if they do not comply, or possibly do not comply with the specifications at the end of the approved shelf life (with the proposed action).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="2">33.</td> <td colspan="4">Minor changes in the manufacture of the finished product</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The overall manufacturing principle remains the same.<br> 2. The new process shall lead to an identical product regarding all aspects of quality, safety and efficacy.<br> 3. The medicinal product does not contain a biological active substance.<br> 4. In case of a change in the sterilisation process, the change is to a pharmacopoeial cycle only.<br> 5. Relevant stability studies in accordance with the relevant guidelines have been started with at least 2 pilot scale or industrial scale batches and at least 3 months stability data are at the disposal of the applicant. Assurance is given that these studies will be finalised and that the data will be provided immediately to the competent authorities if they do not comply, or possibly do not comply with the specifications the end of the approved shelf life (with proposed action).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="8">34.</td> <td colspan="5">Change in the colouring system or the flavouring system currently used in the finished product</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>34.1. reduction or deletion of one or more components</td> <td colspan="3"></td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>34.1.1. the colouring system</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4, 7 (see below)</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>34.1.2. the flavouring system</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4, 7</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>34.2. increase, addition or replacement of one or more components</td> <td colspan="3"></td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>34.2.1. the colouring system</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>34.2.2. the flavouring system</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. No change in the functional characteristics of the pharmaceutical form (for example, disintegration time, dissolution profile).<br> 2. Any minor adjustment to the formulation to maintain the total weight shall be made by an excipient, which currently makes up a major part of the finished product formulation.<br> 3. The finished product specification has only been updated in respect of appearance, odour, taste and, if relevant, - deletion or addition of an identification test.<br> 4. Stability studies (long-term and accelerated) in accordance with the relevant guidelines have been started with at least 2 pilot scale or industrial scale batches and at least 3 months satisfactory stability data are at the disposal of the applicant, as well as the assurance that these studies will be finalised. Data shall be provided immediately to the competent authorities if they do not comply, or possibly do not comply with the specifications the end of the approved shelf life (with the proposed action). In addition, where relevant, photostability testing shall be performed.<br> 5. Any new component shall comply with the colouring matters specified in the list of food additives permitted by the regulatory enactments regarding mandatory safety requirements for food additives.<br> 6. Any new component does not include the use of materials of human or animal origin, for which assessment is required of viral safety data or compliance with the Note for Guidance on minimising the risk of transmitting animal spongiform encephalopathy agents via human and veterinary medicinal products (with the last revision).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="4">35.</td> <td colspan="5">Change in the coating weight or tablets or change in weight of the capsule shells</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>35.1. immediate release oral pharmaceutical forms</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 3, 4 (see below)</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>35.2. modified or prolonged release pharmaceutical forms that dissolve in the intestines</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 2, 3, 4</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The dissolution profile of the new product determined on a minimum of 2 pilot scale batches is comparable to the old one. For herbal medicinal products where dissolution testing may not be feasible, the disintegration time of the new product is comparable to the old one.<br> 2. The coating is not a critical factor for the release mechanism.<br> 3. The finished product specification has only been updated in respect of weight and dimensions.<br> 4. Stability studies in accordance with the relevant guidelines have been started with at least 2 pilot scale or industrial scale batches and at least 3 months satisfactory stability data are at the disposal of the applicant, as well as the assurance that these studies will be finalised. Data shall be provided immediately to the competent authorities if they do not comply, or possibly do not comply with the specifications at the end of the approved shelf life (with the proposed action).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="4">36.</td> <td colspan="5">Change in shape or dimensions of the container or closure</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>36.1. sterile pharmaceutical forms and biological medicinal products</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 2, 3 (see below)</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>36.2. other pharmaceutical forms</td> <td colspan="3">Conditions: 1, 2, 3</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. No change in the qualitative or quantitative composition of the container.<br> 2. The change does not concern a fundamental part of the packaging material, which affects the delivery, use, safety or stability of the finished product.<br> 3. If change is related to the head space or a change in the surface/volume ratio, stability studies in accordance with the relevant guidelines have been started with at least 2 pilot scale (in respect of biological medicinal products - 3) or industrial scale batches and at least 3 months (in respect of biological medicinal products - 6 months) stability data are at the disposal of the applicant of the request. Assurance is given that these studies will be finalised and that the data will be provided immediately to the competent authorities if they do not comply, or possibly do not comply with the specifications at the end of the approved shelf life (with the proposed action).</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="4">37.</td> <td colspan="4">Change in the specification of the finished product</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <td colspan="3">Conditions: 2, 3</td> <td>IB</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td></td> <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The test procedure does not apply to a biological active substance or biological excipient in the medicinal product.</td> </tr>

 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The test procedure does not apply to a biological active substance or biological excipient in the medicinal product.</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td rowspan="2">39.</td> <td colspan="4">Change or addition of the imprints, bossing or other markings (except scoring) on tablets or printing on capsules, including replacement, or addition of inks used for product marking</td> <td>IA</td> </tr>
 <tr>

For herbal medicinal products, where dissolution testing may not be feasible, the disintegration time of the new product is comparable to the old one.<br> 2. Release and end of shelf life specifications of the medicinal product have not been changed (except for dimensions).</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>

The relevant product is not a biological medicinal product.</td> </tr>

 <td rowspan="5">43.</td> <td colspan="5">Addition, replacement or deletion of a measuring or administration device which is not an integrated part of the primary packaging (except spacer devices for metered dose inhalers)</td> </tr>

 <tr>
 <td colspan="5">Conditions: 1. The proposed measuring device shall accurately deliver the required dose for the product concerned in line with the approved posology, and the results of such studies shall be available.<br> 2. The new device is compatible with the medicinal product.<br> 3. The medicinal product can still be accurately delivered.</td> </tr>
 </table>

Notes.

1. The conditions necessary for a given variation to follow either a type IA or a type IB procedure shall be outlined for each subcategory and listed below each variation.

2. To cover any other changes, it shall be necessary to submit applications for any consequential or parallel variations, which may be linked to the change applied for, and to clearly describe the relation between these variations.

3. For notifications, including a certificate of suitability from the European Pharmacopoeia, and when the variation concerns the documentation submitted for the certificate, the documents required for such change shall be submitted to the European Directorate for the Quality of Medicines (EDQM). If the certificate is revised following evaluation of such change, any relevant registration certificates (application dossier) shall be updated. In many cases this may be done through a type I A notification.

4. A biological medicinal product is a product, the active substance of which is a biological substance. A biological substance is produced by or extracted from a biological source, and a combination of physical, chemical and biological testing must be performed for characterisation and the determination of quality thereof, as well as the production process must be controlled. Immunological preparations and medicinal products derived from human blood and human plasma shall be regarded as biological medicinal products.

5. A change in the manufacturing process of a non-proteinaceous component due to a subsequent introduction of a biotechnology step may be made in accordance with the provisions of variations type I No. 15 or No. 20. This specific variation is without prejudice to other variations listed in this Annex, which can be applied in the particular context. The referred to shall not apply to the introduction of a proteinaceous component obtained through a biotechnology process in the formulation of a medicinal product to a medicinal product subject to centralised registration, to which Commission Regulation No 1234/2008 applies.

6. There is no need to notify the State Agency of Medicines of an updated monograph of the European Pharmacopoeia or a national pharmacopoeia of a Member State, if the compliance with the updated monograph is implemented within a time period of 6 months of the publication thereof and reference is made to the current edition in the documentation of the registered medicinal product.

7. The test procedure referred to in this Annex shall have the same meaning as analytical procedure, and the limits shall have the same meaning as acceptance criteria.

Acting for the Minister for Health,
Minister for Culture H. Demakova

[18 May 2010; 3 September 2013]
